# EDU-SERIES build notes

## W3.1 LF02 v5 back-of-head fix (2026-11-04, Film)

User approved this repaint (2026-11-04) after [viewer-check-lf-v3](short/viewer-check-lf-v3.md) failed LF02 on facing direction; the user keeps the v3 look over v4, so the scene, palette, cliff and figure scale are v3's. References viewed again (the three in `projects/edu/EDU/references/web/`): from behind, a bob's back hem sits close to the collar with at most a dark sliver of neck; the hair is one dark mass to the shoulders. Painter: `paintBowedHeadBack` and `paintDreamer` in `src/painted/edu01/set.ts`; nothing else changed.

| Fix (viewer check v3) | Change |
| --- | --- |
| 1 no skin at the nape | Notch under the hem filled in `mix(hair, '#4a3c64', 0.26)` (hair shadow, no skin mixed in) from the hem to the collar; curtains closed to an inner edge of ±0.3 K (the build-notes fallback). |
| 2 back of a bob, not bangs | Back hem shortened to v 0.85–0.91 K and uneven (six points, not mirrored) with short lighter strand tips; side curtains run lower (outer to v 1.1–1.15 K, under the shoulders). |
| 3 show the bow | Whole head rolled 6 degrees about the nape (0, 0.95) K, top toward the cliff edge; crown moved down to (0.08, −0.24) K so the radiating strands fill more of the shape. Dome kept 1.14 × 1.0 K: a flatter 0.92 K dome and a 10 degree roll both exposed a grey crescent of the rig head underlay at the top left (tried, dropped). |
| 4 back-of-shirt cue, soften the flat panel | Opaque (`p.flat` + `solid`) only across the shoulder tops down to 1.05 K under the shoulder line (it must still hide the rig neck and the rig's V-collar line); below that a translucent wash (150) that lets the rig's painted shirt through, a cool shadow wash down the left side; crew collar band (pale rib line plus a darker stitch line 0.09 K under it) and a broken centre back seam. A light wash on the right shoulder was tried and dropped (read as a stain). |
| 5 feet facing away | The rig's front-view foot ovals painted out in the ledge's local tone (sampled from the v3 render, left `#dcc4c3`, right `#dcd2d2`), then a small cool contact shadow, a narrow heel (0.17 × 0.1 K) and a trouser hem over it. Foot centres measured on the v3 render (±0.46 K from `a.feet`, 0.13 K above). |

Casting line: `woman-bob + new: pyjama (shoulder 0.42), P01 back, new: bowed head back (v5)`.

Render: `printf 'EDU1-LF02 0 out/edu01/look/v5/LF02.png\n' | node scripts/stills.mjs src/painted/edu01/look-index.ts`. Determinism: two renders differ by max 1/255 on 7 channels. Caption zone 860–1248 mean 0.78 / p95 0.93 (fail, unchanged from v3; the W5 caption-shade decision stands). ESLint clean on `set.ts` (the unused `skin` parameter was dropped from `paintBowedHeadBack`). Store `projects/edu/EDU/previews/look-frames/ep01/v5/`: `LF02.png`, `LF02-540.png`, `LF02-head-crop.png` (2x native crop x 450–690, y 350–650), `head-crop-v3-v5.png`, `sheet-v3-v5.png` (v3 | v5 at 270 px), `LF02-feet-crop.png` (4x crop x 500–620, y 735–795).

Frame check (viewed: 270 px sheet, 540 px still, 2x head crop, 4x feet crop): the skin notch is gone; at 270 px the head is one dark bob mass set between the shoulders with no face read; at 2x the crown strands, the short uneven back hem over a dark nape and the collar band with the centre seam show. The bow is still moderate: the 6 degree roll and lower crown are visible at 2x and 540 px but at 270 px "looking down" is carried mainly by the pose at the edge, not by the head alone. Feet read as heels under hems at 2x; at 270 px they are too small to matter.

Open: (a) fresh-eyes viewer check of v5 (not by the author); (b) the v3 shoulder cap still juts slightly past the right arm (a squared corner, visible at 2x; geometry unchanged from v3, left for the user's verdict); (c) faint remnants of the rig foot-oval ink at the outer edges at 4x, and the pencil stipple along the lip is interrupted under the feet (not visible at 540 px); (d) kit gap unchanged: a `bowed` back-view head state in `src/painted/kit/back.ts` (Boss, proposed) and a back-view foot (heel) in the rig so shots after SH02 do not repaint the feet.

## W3.1 LF02 v4 dream rework (2026-11-04, Film)

**Dropped by user choice (2026-11-04):** the user picked the v3 picture (the first tile on `sheet-v4.png`) over both v4 looks. LF02 renders the v3 path again (`paintDreamCliff` in `look.tsx`); the v4 code (`paintDreamV4`, `stampCloud`, `paperChip`, `floatRock`, the dream `Spot`, `EDU1-LF02B`) was removed so nothing dead remains; the v4 stills stay in the store as the record. Re-rendered LF02 equals the filed `v3/LF02.png` exactly (pixel diff max 0). Also fixed the two unused-variable ESLint errors in `set.ts` (`N` in `paintLyingHead`, `h` in `paintBedroom`; output unchanged). The section below is the record of the attempt.

User feedback on v3 (via coordinator): what still feels strange is the scene itself; "it is supposed to be a dream". Read again before painting: brief story-first line (the dream is made "of the same paint", read as imagined from the material, CL02), VD04 row (cloud washes, half-painted ledge, **no hard edge on the cliff**), review row S01-B03-SH02 (wide, small figure from behind at an unfinished ledge whose edge melts into paper, looking down into the clouds), concepts C2 (room melts into an abstract watercolour dreamscape), user taste UT01 (place reads at a glance), UT03, UT11. The v3 bowed-head painter is kept unchanged (`paintDreamer` wraps it).

Painter: `paintDreamV4(p, t, look)` in `src/painted/edu01/set.ts`, plus a screen-blended `Spot` halo for `kind="dream"` in `look.tsx` (`DREAM_GLOW` 600, 640, r 460, level 0.3). Shared by both looks:
- wet-in-wet colour blooms (eight `blob` washes, bleed 0.8) instead of horizontal sky bands, so there is no horizon line;
- a glow with no source behind her (four warm-white rings plus the `Spot`);
- the ledge as a floating tongue of paper: pale top face, rose underside that thins in three bleeding layers into cool lilac and a pencil contour, then breaks into drifting paper chips (`paperChip`; three of them float upward);
- small white specks drifting up through the glow; the dim drop straight under the edge kept for height.

| Look | Cloud sea and extras | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `island` (picked, `EDU1-LF02`) | one cloud shape stamped in six rows (`stampCloud`) that grow toward the viewer (scale 0.55 → 1.8), the drop under the edge left open; three rocks floating in the air (`floatRock`, 86/52/34 px) | Reads as a dream at 270 px (floating rocks and ledge, repeated clouds, glow) and still reads as height: the rows shrink toward the edge, so the eye measures the drop. The figure stays the clearest shape. |
| `well` (alternative, `EDU1-LF02B`) | the same cloud stamped in six rings round a glowing hollow far below the edge (a funnel) | Strongly unreal, but the rings mass into a busy foam texture at 270 px that pulls the eye off the figure and reads less as clouds. Kept in the store for the user's comparison. |

Measured (v4 `island`): determinism max 1/255 on 8 channels across two renders; caption zone 860–1248 mean 0.68 / p95 0.88 (fail, as every look; the W5 caption-shade decision stands; lower than v3's 0.78 / 0.93 because the zone now crosses the rose underside). Figure and head unchanged from v3 (hair 128 × 107 px, feet y ~776).

Frame check (viewed): `sheet-v4.png` (v3 | v4 island | v4 well at 270 px) and the 540 px single still. Tried and dropped in this pass: a paper-white `p.flat` under the ledge (read as a flat pale lens); underside washes with bleed 0.3–0.35 (bled about 200 px above the lip into a maroon mound behind her); one rose wash with a straight bottom (hard edge, now three bleeding layers); rocks with a white top and faint cone (read as saucers).

Store: `projects/edu/EDU/previews/look-frames/ep01/v4/` (`LF02.png` = island, `LF02-alt-well.png`, `sheet-v4.png`, `LF02-540.png`).

Open: viewer check of v4 by a fresh invocation; the narrow nape notch from v3 is unchanged (same risk); a darker mottle in the underside's left rose (watercolour pooling) is left as texture; `stampCloud` and `floatRock` are proposed for the kit (ENV layers) at intake if the user keeps the look.

## W3.1 LF02 v3 references: back view, bowed head (2026-11-04, Film)

User correction (via coordinator, 4 Nov 2026): the first v3 head attempt looked very strange (a V-shaped dip under a fringe-like hem read as a face in a V-neck); stop, study real back views, then repaint. References (internal shape study only, never composited) in the store with a sidecar each (source URL, licence, what was learned):

- `projects/edu/EDU/references/web/2026-11-04_bob-rear-view-slight-bow.jpg` (Wikimedia Commons, CC BY-SA 4.0): black jaw-length bob, head tipped slightly forward.
- `projects/edu/EDU/references/web/2026-11-04_bob-rear-view-upright.jpg` (Wikimedia Commons, CC BY-SA 4.0, Alex Neman): upright black bob, crew-neck tee.
- `projects/edu/EDU/references/web/2026-11-04_nape-bowed-updo.jpg` (Wikimedia Commons, CC BY 2.0): hair up, head bowed, nape and ears.

Search note: a free photo of a standing person bowing the head at a ledge, seen from behind, was not found on Commons; a stock-site download was refused by the permission system and not retried. The shapes below come from the three references plus the bow geometry they show.

Shapes to copy (head units `K`, the cast's 4-head proportion kept):

1. **Hair silhouette = a bell, not a dome or helmet:** rounded top narrower than the base, widest at about 70 % of the hair height, a nearly straight horizontal hem with small rounded corners. No arch or split in the hem, no uneven fringe-like ends (those read as a face's bangs).
2. **Crown:** only a short pale parting line from the top edge down about 15 % of the hair height. Never a dot or whorl in the middle of the mass (read as an eye in v1/v2).
3. **Strands:** long near-vertical streaks, gently converging toward the parting at the top, fine light highlights in the lower half on the lit side; the mass stays one even dark value.
4. **Neck and nape:** under the hem a neck column narrower than the hair (about 0.45 of the hair width), in shadowed skin. Upright, almost no neck shows (hem nearly on the collar); bowed, the nape lengthens to about 0.15–0.2 of the hair height. Its sides run into the collar; never a square patch.
5. **Collar:** a shallow U crew curve at the neck base. A V reads as the front of a shirt.
6. **Shoulders:** they slope down and out from the collar (trapezius, about 15–20 degrees), then round over into the arms; real head width is about 0.6 of the shoulder width (the 4-head cast keeps its larger head). Rounded/hunched shoulders lift their inner tops slightly so they overlap the lower corners of the hair: the head sits down between them.
7. **The bow itself:** the head's top drops (crown lower relative to the shoulders than upright) while the hem stays horizontal; the cue is the longer nape plus the head sunk between the shoulders, not a tilt of the hair mass.

Second user correction (via coordinator, 4 Nov 2026): the repaint from points 1–7 "still looks strange"; study the perspective of a bowed head before painting. Points 4 and 7 above are superseded by the construction sheet below (a bowed head shows a **short** neck, not a longer one, and the bob hem does not stay level).

### LF02 v3 bowed-head construction sheet

Sketch: store `projects/edu/EDU/previews/look-frames/ep01/v3/construction-sketch.png` (viewed; side view above, back view below, head units `K`). Painter: `paintBowedHeadBack` in `src/painted/edu01/set.ts` draws the whole head from these numbers (the rig head is only an underlay hidden by it, like `paintLyingHead` in LF01).

1. **Side view, tilt:** skull = ellipse 1.0 × 1.15 K, long axis tilted 40 degrees forward (face down toward the drop). The crown rotates toward the face and down; the occiput and nape now face the camera behind her. The neck leans forward from the shoulders to the skull base, so from behind it is foreshortened.
2. **Back view, skull and hair silhouette:** hair dome = half ellipse 1.12 × 1.0 K centred at the head centre; sides widen to ±1.2 K at v +0.3; below that the bob splits: the **side hair falls forward** past the jaw and hangs as two curtains to v +1.05 (outer ±1.0–1.18 K, inner ±0.46–0.62 K), while the **back hair ends higher** because the hem swings forward off the tipped skull.
3. **Crown:** at (0.06, −0.42) K, inside the mass (no longer on the top edge, because it rotated toward the camera). Strands radiate from it with no dot: up and over the top edge, sideways out to the edge then down the curtains (the forward fall), and down to the nape hairline (short, bulging with the occiput).
4. **Nape hairline = lowest edge of the back hair:** a soft W at v +0.66–0.72 between ±0.46 K, with two small points of hair at ±0.3 K.
5. **Neck:** a short skin column ±0.45–0.5 K from the hairline to the collar (visible length about 0.4–0.5 K, shorter than upright because it leans away), its top in the shadow of the hair mass (a cool wash v +0.6–0.85).
6. **Ears:** move up and back relative to the shoulders, but the forward-falling bob curtains cover them in this cut; not drawn (an ear would read as a handle at 27 px of head at 270 px).
7. **Shoulders and collar:** shoulder line at v +0.9 K (the head sinks so the nape hairline sits just above it); a shallow U crew collar dipping to v +1.2 K; the shoulders are nearer the camera than the forward head, so they overlap the curtain ends, then slope down and out about 15–20 degrees into the arms. For this shot the cast shoulder is widened 0.34 → 0.42 (`SLEEPER` copy in `paintDreamCliff`) so the shoulders are wider than the hair, as in the references.
8. **Light:** key from the upper right (`PAPER_LIGHT`): strands on the right half lighter; the shadow under the hair falls on the nape and the shoulder tops.

### LF02 v3 and LF04 v3 result

Render: `printf 'EDU1-LF02 0 out/edu01/look/v3/LF02.png\nEDU1-LF04 0 out/edu01/look/v3/LF04.png\n' | node scripts/stills.mjs src/painted/edu01/look-index.ts` (one Chrome). Store: `projects/edu/EDU/previews/look-frames/ep01/v3/` (LF02, LF04, `sheet-v3.png` = v2 LF02 | v3 LF02 | v3 LF04 at 270 px, `construction-sketch.png`, `LF02-head-crop.png` = 2x native crop of the head).

| Look | Change | Casting line | Measured |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| LF02 | Bowed head per the construction sheet (`paintBowedHeadBack` draws the whole head; rig `nod [-0.18, -1.02]` keeps the rig head under it and centred on the torso, which `lean 0.1` had pushed 14 px off); cast shoulder 0.34 → 0.42 for this shot; shoulder caps and crew collar in the rig's lit shirt tone (`toneOf`, opaque). Strata: the four ruled pencil dashes became three broken contours that bend toward the edge (two pieces each). Cliff edge, bare paper and cloud depth unchanged from v2. | `woman-bob + new: pyjama (shoulder 0.42), P01 back, new: bowed head back` | hair 128 × 107 px (x 506–634, y 381–488; v2 head 106 px tall); figure hair top y 381 to feet y ~776 (395 px); determinism: two renders differ by max 1/255 on 3 channels; caption zone 860–1248 mean 0.78 / p95 0.93 (fail, unchanged decision: caption shade at W5) |
| LF04 | Dumbbell floor shadow moved up under the plates' bottom rims and widened (`ell(x + 16, y + 60, 178, 16)`), so both plates rest on it | `new: icons x4` | caption zone mean 0.90 / p95 0.91 (fail, same decision) |

Frame check (viewed): 270 px sheet `sheet-v3.png` and the 540 px single still. At 270 px the head now reads as a dark bob seen from behind, set down between rounded shoulders, with no dot or pale square; the strata no longer read as text lines. At 540 px the crown's radiating strands and the forward-falling side curtains show; the narrow shadowed nape between the curtains is the remaining risk (a small skin notch under dark hair can still be taken for a face in shadow at 270 px); the bow itself is moderate, carried by the sunk head rather than a visible tilt.

Tried and dropped (same session): (a) a full-head overlay with a V-shaped dip under an uneven hem (read as a face in a V-neck); (b) a broad shirt panel over the whole upper torso (flat colour mismatch, read as a bib or scarf; `solid` alone let the rig neck show through, so the cap now gets `p.flat` first); (c) shoulder caps narrower than their inner collar points (crossed into pointed "ears").

Open: a fresh-eyes viewer check of v3 (W3.1 rule: never author and check in one invocation); if the nape still reads as a face, the next step is to close the curtains further (inner edge ±0.3 K) so only a sliver of neck shows. Kit gap (Boss, proposed): a `bowed` back-view head state in `src/painted/kit/back.ts` built from this sheet, so W5 plates and the B03 shots after SH02 do not need the per-shot painter.

- **Lead size still small in LF02** (wide by design, row "small figure on a ledge"): the bowed head reads at 540 px, weaker at 270 px; left for the user.

| Look | First shot | Casting line / kit layers | Lead (measured from the still, px) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| LF01 bedroom | S01-B01-SH01 | `woman-bob + new: pyjama, P36, sleepy`; kit: ENV-SKY moon disc (`paintSkyDisc`), `Spot` (clock glow overlay, 0.26); `new: bedroom set` | lying figure about 820 px long at U 520 (est 1.58 m); face centre about y 790, above the band |
| LF02 dream cliff | S01-B03-SH02 | `woman-bob + new: pyjama, P01 (back view, nod down)`; `new: cloud-dream plate` with pencil under-drawing below the painted cliff | standing figure about 400 px at U 250 (wide) |
| LF03 idea space | S01-B05-SH01 | `woman-bob + new: pyjama, P31, worried (eyes up-right)`; `new: idea-space ground`, `new: bed-edge vignette`, brush "?" | seated figure about 690 px at U 520; face centre about y 760 |
| LF04 trigger icons | S01-B06-SH01 + SH02 end | `new: icons x4` (cup, scribble ball, moon + clock 1:50, dumbbell) | icons about 300 px; row y 360–720 (steam to the cup top), dumbbell y 1360–1560 |
| LF05 doctor | S01-B07-SH03 | `woman-pixie + doctor, P01, happy` (kit, unchanged); `new: doctor vignette ground` (window light, plant shelf) | figure 1.57 m at U 980, cut at the shins; face centre about y 600 |

Findings:
- **Caption zone** (`scripts/caption-zone.py`, default white-outline thresholds): every look fails (LF01 mean 0.36 / p95 0.68 from the lit duvet in the band; LF02–LF05 mean 0.72–0.90 on paper/cloud). The brief sets no caption colour. Needs a decision before W5 (Editor/coordinator, user): dark-fill captions on the paper and dream grounds, or the kit `CaptionShade` band while a caption is up (as EG-EP02).
- **Determinism:** LF01 and LF03 rendered twice: max channel difference 1/255 on 0.0001 % of channels (LF01), identical (LF03).
- **Washes vs objects:** shaped objects (blanket, mattress, curtains, rug) need an opaque `p.flat` underlayer before the textured passes; `washAt` alone left them translucent (mattress line visible through the blanket) and large `washAt` bleeds turned the rug and moon pool into blobs. Helper `body()` in `set.ts`.
- **Amber over blue reads green:** a thin `paintHalo` in `#e8a35c` over the ink-blue wall painted a green patch; the clock glow is the wall colour mixed toward amber plus a screen-blend `Spot`.
- **P36 lying head:** the kit's rotated profile head reads as a face-up head on the pillow at 270 px, but the hair mass sits under the head and the half-lid eye reads fairly open; left to the viewer check.
- **Pyjama:** long trousers instead of the brief's shorts (rig has no shorts); open question for the user.
- **Viewer check (2026-11-04, fresh Film):** LF01 fail (sleeper's head/face unreadable, no female cue, rug fills the lower 40 %), LF02 fail (no look-down, cliff not "unfinished", no sense of height), LF03 pass, LF04 pass (dumbbell reads as "H"), LF05 fail (doctor reads male, no stethoscope). Details and fixes: [viewer-check-lf-v1](short/viewer-check-lf-v1.md).
- Not done in this pass: the [thumbnails](../../docs/sequence-workflow.md#look-thumbnails) step (skipped on coordinator instruction) and the [viewer check](../../docs/sequence-workflow.md#viewer-check) (needs a fresh Film invocation).

| Look | Shot (frame) | Casting line / kit layers | Lead / subject (projected from the camera, px) | Caption band 860–1248 (`caption-zone.py`, no shade) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| LF01 kitchen | S01-B01-SH02 (f24 of 48) | `woman-curly + new: kitchen-apron, P07 (knife raised in the right hand), smug`, front view squared to the lens, `headCock 6`; kit: ENV-GLOW (`paintHalo`) pendant and window sun; `new: SET-KITCHEN`, `new: knife` | head top y 243 → island back edge y 844: visible cook 561 px (521 at f0, 606 at f47); eyes y 406; onion top y 788 (838 at f47); board edge in the band (allowed) | mean 0.614, p95 0.87: fail |
| LF02 cell macro | S01-B03-SH01 (f110 of 126) | `new: cell-macro set` (CELL space): beads, teal sac with notched enzymes, neighbours at 0.24 strength | hero cell y 263–703 (f110), 448–646 at f0 | mean 0.854, p95 0.902: fail (brightest ground of the episode) |
| LF03 eye macro | S01-B04-SH02 (f45 of 102) | `new: eye-macro plate` (EYE space): the cook's left eye, gold nerve lines + sparks, lilac gas veils, tear wave (from f53), drop (from f87) | eye opening about x 75–1000, y 330–760; centre y 560 | mean 0.406, p95 0.441: fail |

Spikes (brief "smallest spikes"):
- **1 Cell model reads at 270 px:** pass for SH01 (beads and sac separate and nameable at 270 px on `sheet-LF02.png`; the hero is the only full-colour cell). SH02 key state (`EDU2-SPK-CELL`): the open sac and orange beads next to teal enzymes read; the lilac mist is faint and the blade is a plain grey plane; both to strengthen at W5.2.
- **2 Eye macro:** reads as an eye at a glance (UT01/UT02). Sparks read as gold stars at f45; the tear wave reads as a pale band sweeping across (f71), more a band than water; open for the viewer check.
- **5 Captions and lead:** numbers above. No face, onion, eye or doodle in the band on the look frames (LF01 onion top 838 at the last frame, 2 px clear).

Findings:
- **Washes only darken.** A light colour laid as `washAt` over a darker ground (gold light on the island, lilac gas on brown skin) painted a brown or grey smudge. Light patches and the gas are now flat veils (`hardFill` on `p.layer(alpha)`), stacked at two or three sizes for a soft edge. A light colour needs enough blue to stay lilac over brown skin (`#9a80ff` at 0.3 per layer).
- **Macro sets and the near plane.** `YCAM_NEAR` is 0.3 m, so EYE (set-m ≈ 1 cm, camera 0.3 m away) clipped everything: the first LF03 render was plain skin. The eye is projected through a ×10 copy of the set (same picture); CELL clears the near plane as authored.
- **Cell depth.** With the authored 0.25 cell depth, each cell's front ring and back face separated by the parallax and read as stray lines; drawn at ±0.06.
- **LF01 framing.** At the brief's `r 1.9, f 1000` the island front and an empty island top filled the lower half and the lead measured 441 px; the longer lens from further back fixes both (brief R1.1). The window sits just off the left edge on this arc, so the key reads only as the warm rim and the light patches (UT04 risk for the viewer check).
- **Sclera shade bug** (fixed): the upper-lid shadow on the eyeball was not clipped to the eye opening and painted a beige box over the lid.
- **Determinism:** LF01 f24 and LF03 f45 rendered twice: max channel difference 9/255, PSNR 76.6 dB (run noise per DF-20261030-render-run-noise; no flicker source found).
- Not done in this pass: [thumbnails](../../docs/sequence-workflow.md#look-thumbnails) (skipped: the user asked for the look frames now, coordinator instruction) and the [viewer check](../../docs/sequence-workflow.md#viewer-check) (needs a fresh Film invocation that did not paint them).

Caption zone 860–1248 (no shade): mean 0.572, p95 0.745, fail |
| LF02 cell macro | Sac rim faint at small size → hero sac ink 1.4 → 2.1 px (one line) | mean 0.854, p95 0.902, fail (unchanged ground) |

Hard mist blobs → four soft translucent wisps (7 stacked layers, `#8a6cff` at 0.08 each, tapered to nothing at the top) drawn in toward the lower lid. Caption zone: mean 0.397, p95 0.434, fail |

Findings:
- **Counter top vs. lead size is geometry.** With a 1.62 m cook behind a 0.90 m island, the island top only drops into the lower third when head-to-waist (0.74 m) spans ~1050 px, i.e. a medium close-up (`f/d ≈ 1100–1450`). An r 1.55 / f 1400 trial at her eye height still left the island top as an empty lower 40%. The board had to move onto the cook-camera line or the onion left frame right.
- **Soft light on dark skin.** A shape stacked at shrinking sizes on `p.layer(a/n)` (`softVeil` in `macro.ts`) gives a soft edge without the wash's dark border; 8–14 steps are needed on large shapes or the steps show as rings.
- **Wisps that cross** stack alpha into bright lilac knots (still visible at f71 / f101 where two cross); sway kept at 0.04 set-m.
- Open, not in the r1 findings: the kit `woman-curly` hair shows a lighter flap at the upper left of the head (also in v1; kit/Boss); at f101 the tear sheet still dims the iris and at f71 a small dark smudge sits above the upper lid near the centre (W5.2 polish); determinism not re-measured for v2.

New soft veils inside the frame: nose-side plane on the left third down to a lit nose wing with its crease at the bottom-left, tear trough under the lower lid, lit lid puff, cheekbone light lower right (two stacked ovals), cheek shade at the bottom, forehead light; 520 pores / freckles as tiny low-alpha fills; two under-eye lines | visible at 270 px on the sheet (nose-side band, cheek light, trough) |
| Mist reads as thin straight beams with bright tips | Three wisps, each 22 overlapping lens-shaped strokes along a curling centre line (normal-offset, taper to nothing at both ends), width 0.051 → 0.006 set-m bottom to top, alpha fading from v 0.7, gone by set y −0.07 (below the lower lid at −0.05); `#9358ec` at 0.26 per stroke over 4 soft steps; wisps spaced (x0 −0.095 / 0.015 / 0.115) and sway 0.026 so they do not cross into knots; mist fades with the tear wave (×0.45 by the end) | purple reads at 270 px at f0 / f45; no bright tip |
| Nerves read as cracks / twigs | Core lines 0.6–0.9 px mixed toward the sclera (a sub-layer alpha fades ink toward the brown paper, which made them brown twigs); three short branches; glow only while a spark runs | barely visible at rest, gold on the spark (f45) |
| Brow a hard band | Filled brow → faint soft underlay + 110 three-point brush hairs | – |

Caption zone (no shade, band 860–1248): f0 / f45 / f71 / f101 mean 0.380 / 0.379 / 0.388 / 0.383, p95 0.409 / 0.409 / 0.520 / 0.412, fail (v2 0.397 / 0.434): the shade question stays open.

Findings:
- **Eye at 40% conflicts with a centred caption.** `position: 'middle'` centres the caption block at y 960 (line height 51 × 1.65 ≈ 84 px: one line 918–1002, two 876–1044), so any eye centred at 40% with a ~410 px opening puts the caption on the lower lid. The brief's band 860–1248 matches a bottom-anchored block (`position: 'lower'`, bottom edge y 1240, the production-requirements default of a bottom edge near 1250–1344): two lines 1072–1240 clear the eye (bottom ≤ 992) by ~80 px. `captionStyle` is video-level, so this is a deliverable decision (open; brief R1.3). Both EDU briefs name `'middle'` with the 860–1248 band; proposed for the user's review: align the brief text with the runtime anchor.
- **Low-alpha fills vanish.** Veils at ≤ ~0.03 per fill (e.g. 0.05 over 5 steps) did not show at all over the skin; ~0.065–0.1 per fill is the working range for the mist.
- **Circular puffs band; lens strokes do not.** A chain of round soft puffs along the wisp showed horizontal scallops (each puff's top and bottom edge); lens strokes that taper along the line hide their ends.
- Open (not in r2): the tear sheet at f71 is still an opaque grey band over the iris (W5.2 polish, noted in v2); determinism not re-measured.

LF01 polish (episode code only, kit unchanged): `KITCHEN_LIGHT` rim `#f6cf86` mix 0.45 depth 0.09 → `#ffb84d` 0.4 / 0.05 and lit glaze `#fbe7bf` 0.25 / 95 → `#ffbe6a` 0.22 / 70 (the "muddy smudge" was the window rim in opaque passes on the near-black `woman-curly` cloud; it is now a thin gold rim); the cook's left arm (screen right) bends at the waist with the open hand on the island top (`lArm` via/to from `shoulderPoint`, CP-P arm depth) instead of hanging straight; the island top gets plank tones, dry grain strokes, the cook's soft shadow and a darker near edge; the board gets tonal streaks, grain, knife scores, a worn far edge and two onion-skin flakes.

Measured (v5): eye opening (pixels L > 100 with b ≥ r − 6, y 400–1400) f0 / f45 / f71 / f101 x 92–1006 / 68–1016 / 52–1018 / 46–1018 → width 84.6 / 87.8 / 89.4 / 90.0%, centre y 856 / 855 / 864 / 884 (44.6 / 44.5 / 45.0 / 46.0%), same as v4; lower lashes end at y 1150–1168 (above the 1176 band top). Caption zone band 1176–1344 (`caption-zone.py --band 1176:1344`): mean 0.366 / 0.373 / 0.372 / 0.369, p95 0.404 / 0.409 / 0.405 / 0.404; fails the 0.25 default as in v4 (skin only; the left wisp's curl touches the band at x < 150, outside the caption box), so the shade decision stays open. Determinism: f45 rendered twice, PSNR 66.6 dB (not byte-identical; no visible change). `tsc` (no edu02 errors; three pre-existing test errors in `tests/loudness.test.ts`, `tests/render-options.test.ts`) and `eslint src/painted/edu02/` clean.

Findings:
- **Globe points crop to ticks at the lids.** At this close camera a point on the globe (z up to 0.12) projects outside the skin-plane opening that clips it; a long nerve must be laid on the opening plane (or clipped by its projection), not on `onGlobe`.
- **Low-alpha veils add up less than their sum.** Fifteen overlapping 0.03 veils read near 0.1; the mist needed 0.05–0.08 per veil. Tune by looking, not by arithmetic.
- **Soft but defined vapour needs an edge, not a blur.** Every fully soft mist (v2–v4) read as a smudge; a ribbon with soft widths plus one thin lit edge on the curl reads as a wisp.

Open: fresh viewer check on LF01 v5 and LF03 v5 (coordinator); caption shade for the 1176–1344 band; the dark limbal ring arcs (v4, kept because r4 praised the iris) may read as outline segments at the left and right of the iris; the tear wave is quiet at 270 px (clear by design); Studio seek not checked (look stills only, no timeline yet).

The resting hand sits in the band (y ≈ 1100–1260; hands are not in the frame-zone list). Findings:
- **A hand on a counter in front of a figure needs its own item.** The rig paints the figure as one item behind the island, so any hand target below the island's far edge is painted over; repaint the forearm and hand as a separate depth-sorted item on the counter, with the same shape calls.
- **Washes with bleed cross edges.** A wall wash at bleed 0.3 crossed the room corner and dried to a blotch on the other wall; a cook shadow at bleed 0.45 spread over the cabinet behind the island. Use flat veils or bleed ≤ 0.08 for washes that must stop at an edge.
- **Vapour reads from filaments, not from one soft ribbon.** One tapering ribbon reads as a tube however soft; several low-alpha strands drifting across each other, widening as they rise, read as smoke.

Open: fresh viewer check on LF01 v6 and LF03 v6 (coordinator); **Film**: the Ø 0.14 onion and the moved `T-ONION` change the estimated framing of B01-SH01, B02-SH01/SH02, B06-SH04, B07-SH01 (onion ~1.75x larger at the same camera; the brief scale baseline says Ø 0.08) — keep the set change or ask for a per-shot hero scale; caption shade for both bands; the f71 tear-wave refraction line still crosses the pupil (v5, unchanged); the third LF03 wisp barely shows; Studio seek not checked (look stills only, no timeline).

## EDU-EP02-SHORT W4.2 final narration (Editor, 5 Nov 2026)
- One Gemini call (Kore, same style as the audition, `--text-file` with ellipses as commas): raw 68.0 s. Pauses >=0.25 s shortened (0.15 + 0.35 x gap), atempo 1.05, loudnorm -16 LUFS: 59.58 s. Whisper (large-v3-turbo) dropped L11-L13 on this read, so line times come from silence boundaries in script order (+-0.15 s), Whisper only as cross-check. Reproduce: out/edu02/narration/tts-text.txt; store `projects/edu/EDU/narration/ep02/`.
- Kore reads far slower than the script's ~6 syl/s estimate: speech 59.3 s vs ~40 s estimated. 1 Gemini request used.
- W4.2 redo (tempo 1.16, Editor, 5 Nov 2026): user kept the script and asked for a faster read. Same cached raw take (no Gemini request); same pause shortening (0.15 + 0.35 x gap for gaps >= 0.25 s), atempo 1.16 instead of 1.05 (1.05 x 62.3 s shortened speech / 54.5 s target gives a minimum of 1.143), loudnorm -16 LUFS. Speech 0.20-54.07 s, file 54.35 s, video about 59.8 s. Clean: peak -1.5 dBFS, no clipping, Whisper large-v3-turbo read all 17 lines. Gap build script was a scratch rebuild of `narration.py` `silent_runs`/`shorten` (threshold 300, 0.25 s) over `out/edu02/narration/raw-kore.wav`. Store `projects/edu/EDU/narration/ep02/2026-11-05_ep02-narration-kore-t1.16.wav` (the 1.05 file stays beside it). Margin to 60 s is ~0.2 s; the sob/sting gap total is a plan figure, re-measure when built.
- W4.2 redo pass 3 (`gemini-fast`, Editor, 5 Nov 2026): user asked for a new Kore read where the model itself speaks faster instead of atempo. One Gemini request (cache key 9dbf16b6f1921118, `--text-file` same tts-text.txt, style "fast-paced, quick brisk delivery with short pauses between phrases, playful deadpan female narrator, dry and matter-of-fact, speaking quickly without dragging"): raw 54.16 s with speech 0.25-53.97 s, so no atempo and no pause shortening were needed (the old style already said "brisk"; adding explicit pace and short pauses cut the raw read from 68.0 s to 54.2 s). Level: per-line LUFS spread was -13.4..-17.4 (short lines louder), each of 17 line slices got a gain to -16 LUFS (steps at gap centres, 40 ms smoothing) then `alimiter` 0.82; result -15.9..-16.4 per line, -16.3 integrated, peak -1.7 dBFS. Whisper (venv .venv-whisper needs the audio passed as a numpy array: its PyAV rejects `metadata_errors`) dropped L08-L10 and part of L11, so timing came from silence gaps in script order (30 gaps matched 17 lines). Leveling and Whisper scratch scripts were in the session scratchpad. Store `projects/edu/EDU/narration/ep02/2026-11-05_ep02-narration-kore-fast.wav` (raw `..._raw-kore-fast.wav/.json`); the t1.16 and 1.05 files stay beside it.

- **Boss interpretation (for Film):** the blade edge runs 50° up to the right through the tear at the hero's upper-left wall, strikes in l 0–12 and slides out along its edge l 18–34 (the sheet gives only the entry); the tear is a jagged rip across the top wall line; the met beads turn orange and leave as lilac (gas), the other five beads stay yellow.
- **Caption band:** `caption-zone.py --band 1176:1344` p95 0.88–0.93 on all 24 frames (cream cell layer), so every B03 caption needs the shade (Editor).

## EP02 W5.2 group G-C plates (2026-11-05, Boss)

Determinism: B04-SH01 l46 and B04-SH03 l47 rendered twice, PSNR 75.2 / 73.4 dB.

- **N6 polish (eye.ts, carried from the LF03 v6 accept):** upper-lid shadow reaches 0.034 set-m with the four bands nearest the lid at 0.15 (was 0.026, 0.095); nerve halo seven widths to ~9x the core + 8 px, total ≤ 0.29 (glow cap); tear wave: body feathered (stacked narrowing bands, 5 × 0.014 + 4 × 0.011 instead of flat 0.05 + 0.04), crest and lead line at 30 % inside 0.026 set-m of the pupil centre (full from 0.042), lead line 0.3 / 2 px so the wave shows over the white; mist haze and filaments wider and fainter; the third wisp's curl was below the frame (y −0.225 vs bottom ≈ −0.215) and now ends at −0.175. Body: haze, puffs strung along the path (kit fog-bank puffs retinted `#9d6bff`), three filaments; densest ≈ 0.55. The onion→face path lies almost on the lens axis at yaw −35, so its picture shape comes from sideways lean terms (±0.055–0.06 m) and the curls; v1 without them was one purple column.
- **N7 waterfall tears (kitchen.ts `CookState.waterfall`, `paintWaterfallTears`):** two jets from (±0.44, 0.2) head units, thrown out to ±2.0 K about level, landing on the bib at the shoulders, wobbling width 0.05 → 0.16 K, spray veil, blue underside, lit core, flow streaks, beads off the outer edge, wet patch and splash at the landing. v1 (rising arcs, even width, dark edge line) read as a stethoscope/tube.
- **N4 slices (kitchen.ts `OnionPiece` `slices`):** flat half-discs with a side band, ring face and contact shadow, one per chop from (−0.30, 0.10) along +x.
- **Head frame:** the hair-rim head frame (`Kpx`, centre, head-cock rotation) is now `headFrame()` shared by the rim and the tears; LF01 numbers unchanged.

Measured (`metricsOf`, px): SH01 l0 O2 centre 616 (Ø 148), eyes 358 (x 523), head top 200, island far edge 669 (lead 469); l36 chin 676; l46 eyes 589; l52 eyes 600, head top 383, chin 754, island far 1102 (lead 719), O2 tip 1163 / body 1212–1485 / centre 1357, board top y 1177–1500, resting fist ≈ (274, 1130), knife tip (320, 1592). SH03 eyes 620; head top 434 → 403; chin 752 → 774; lead 610 → 718; O2 centre 1174 → 1377, board 1092/1196 → off the bottom. Caption band 1176–1344 (`caption-zone.py`): SH01 p95 0.87 / 0.87 / 0.71 / 0.92 / 0.92 (l 0/24/36/46/52), SH02 0.40–0.41 (mean 0.37) on 7 frames, SH03 0.92 on 6 frames (board and onion under the band): SH01 and SH03 captions need the shade (Editor).

Findings:
- **A path along the lens axis has no picture shape.** Vapour or any travel from a near prop to a face behind it, filmed from that line, projects as a straight vertical column; give it sideways lean in camera-right metres and end features (curls) to read.
- **White on white does not show water.** On the sclera a white crest is invisible; the wave reads by its dark leading line, so that line carries the wave there and fades over the pupil instead.
- **Rock the knife on its tip for a chop.** Lifting the whole knife kept the blade near level and, from a three-quarter camera, read as a stab across the body; swinging the grip 23° → 55° about the tip on the board reads as chopping.
## EP02 W5.2 group G-D plates (2026-11-05, Boss)

Shots S01-B05-SH01 (53 f), SH02 (122 f), SH03 (104 f), SH04 (32 f), SH05 (48 f) as `plateOf(id, f)` in `src/painted/edu02/shots.tsx`; new modules `props.ts` (N8 bread `paintBreadInMouth`, candle/matchbox `candleItems`, `paintMatch`, N4 frost `paintFrost`, N2 fridge-POV interior `fridgePovItems` + `paintFridgeDark`, front wall `paintFrontWall`) and `doodles.ts` (N9 `paintDoodle`: chalk "?" drawn on, red "✗" stamped). `world.ts` now exports `hull`. Evidence: `projects/edu/EDU/previews/shots/ep02/w52-GD/` (`sheet-SH01.png` … `sheet-SH05.png`, 30 full-size frames, `ab.png` SH02 mist crops), each sheet viewed. Determinism: SH02 l48 and SH04 l16 rendered twice, PSNR 75.9 / 80.0 dB.

- **CookState** (kitchen.ts) gains `at` (cook position; SH04 stands her at the fridge), `rPose`/`lPose` (kit `poseMix` blends: P02 → P24 wipe, P06 shrug), `lRest`, `rReach` (right hand to a world point), `bread {bob, tilt}`, `cold`. `KitchenOpts` gains `knifeRest` (hand-free knife on the board), `candle`, `fridgePov {door, light}`, `carryR {at, frost}`; `OnionPiece` gains `frost`; `KitchenMist` gains `via` (quadratic path) and `mist` accepts an array (SH03 two wisps).
- **Mist (SH02, SH03, SH05):** every wisp is a depth-sorted item at its source (fog depth gate, DF-20261030-painted-fog-front-depth-gate). SH02's `via` bends the path camera-right of the bread so it reads as going around the bread to the eyes (`ab.png`, l30/l48).
- **SH04 projection:** camera at the fridge back (z −1.45 → −1.40, hy 760, `near` 0.04); her feet are inside the projected frame but the crisper front panel (alpha 0.72) and the lower shelf cover the legs, so the picture is head-to-waist over the shelf; the door swings 0 → 125° and sorts in front of her below 50°.

Feasibility and value changes (Boss, AI13; recorded in the s01-b05 change log for Film): bread 0.11 → 0.17 m (at 0.11 the 4-head rig's mouth hid it); match in the left hand (right-hand reach across the body to the candle read stiff); candle base 0.915 / top 1.05 / wick 1.065 m on a saucer at (−0.02, 0.9, 0.04); O3 path (1.88, 1.02, −1.0) → (1.87, 1.1, −0.72) over l14–28; ✗ at (860, 790) size 130 (sheet position sat on her cheek); SH05 dolly end r 1.50 → 1.55 (head height 286 px vs SH03 263 px, +8.7 %, inside the ±10 % rhyme; at 1.50 it was +12.5 %).

Measured (`metricsOf`, px): SH01 head top 378, eyes 531 (x 658 → 452 on the truck), chin 639, island far 858 (lead 480), onion Ø 141 at y 907. SH02 l0 → l100 head top 438 → 393, eyes 620, chin 749 → 782, lead 591 → 761, onion centre 1134 → 1415 (base 1221 → 1549). SH03 l0 → l95 head top 310 → 318, eyes 469 → 472, chin 581, lead 486 → 477, head 263, onion centre 820. SH04 l0 → l31 head top 301 → 277, eyes 535 → 523, chin 701 → 698, head 400 → 421. SH05 l0 → l47 head top 378 → 360, chin 639 → 647, lead 477 → 537, head 261 → 287, onion 904 → 967. SH02 vs caption band 1176–1344: eyes 620, bread ≈ 690–820 (l48 crop) and ✗ 725–855 above it; the onion halves (centre 1236 at l48), the knife's chopping stroke and the board front sit in or under it. Caption band (`caption-zone.py --band 1176:1344`) p95: SH01 0.62–0.87, SH02 0.92, SH03 0.87, SH04 0.28 (dark f0) then 0.95–0.97, SH05 0.86 → 0.65; every frame fails, so every spoken G-D shot needs the shade (W5.3, Editor).

Findings:
- **`tintPainter` reaches only `p.wash` / `p.flat` / `p.ink`.** `hardFill`, `washAt`, `inkLine`, `solid` and the kit rig draw with brush directly, so a tinted painter leaves them unchanged: G-A's `cookSoft` tint is a no-op. Tint at source (the onion `tint` parameter) or paint a veil over the result (`paintFridgeDark`, the cold veil).
- **Brush ink lines are translucent.** A keyline under an ink stroke shows through and the chalk "?" read grey; screen-space doodles are opaque filled ribbons (key at 1.55 w, colour at w).
- **A cross drawn stroke by stroke reads as a tick** for the first half; a stamp (whole at once, 4 f pop) reads as ✗.
- **Kitchen wash flicker under a moving camera (not fixed):** the island near-edge washes (seeds 41171/41172) and the fridge wash (40834) change their bleed shape between frames when the camera moves, visible as brown smears (SH01 l16/l52, SH02 l30/l102, SH03 l28/l70; the same in G-A B01-SH02). Fixing it changes the approved LF01 plate, so it waits for a decision.

Not done: no motion render or seek pass of the five shots (stills only), sound not checked, no commit.

Evidence: `projects/edu/EDU/previews/shots/ep02/w52-GE/` (`sheet-sh01.png` … `sheet-sh04.png`, `pair-B02SH02l0-B06SH04l0.png`, 29 full-size frames), each sheet viewed. Determinism: SH03 l30 and SH04 l77 rendered twice, PSNR 72.2 / 79.2 dB. Regression (shared kitchen.ts): B05-SH03 l95 74.3 dB, B05-SH02 l48 75.8 dB, B02-SH02 l0 82.9 dB against the stored G-D / G-A frames (unchanged; absent options keep the old paths).

- **N8 swim goggles (props.ts `paintGoggles`, `CookState.goggles`):** painted after the figure in the rig's front head frame (`headFrame`, eyes at head units (±0.4, 0.05)): cyan ring lenses (quads, lit upper-left), pale-blue glass veil 0.24 with a thin sheen streak upper-left only (glass-sheen veil finding: the eyes read through), bridge, strap to the curl cloud's edge with two curl bulges either side of it; `lift` (head units above the eyes), `held` (strap pulled up over the curls), `squash`, `bounce`.
- **N4 O4 (kitchen.ts `OnionPiece` `dome`, `paintOnionDome`):** a half onion lying cut face down (1.06 r high and wide, 0.92 r half-axis), skin longitudes stem to root, neck stub and root tuft; `cut {s, depth, fall}` splits it at the slice plane once the blade is in, so the order is rest → blade → slice (the slice's skin hides the blade inside the cut), the groove over the top, and the ringed cut face when the slice falls off.
- **Kit-level options:** `KitchenOpts.cullNear` (items whose anchor is nearer than this are skipped, the island kept); `KitchenMist.n` / `width` (a single thin thread); `CookState.lPose` takes `b`/`u`, `mirror` (the kit pose's right arm reflected: the left-hand P24 eye wipe) and `off`.
- **SH03 camera `near` 0.04:** at the default 0.3 the chop grip and tip (0.2–0.3 m from the lens) did not project and the right arm fell back to P07's raised wave.

Measured (`metricsOf`, px): SH01 head top 458 → 420, eyes 620, chin 736 → 762, island far 978 → 1077 (lead 520 → 657, ≥ 480), onion (O2 halves at T-ONION) centre 1058 → 1261, base 1132 → 1369. SH02 head top 402 → 383, chin 775 → 789, island far 1128 → 1187 (lead 726 → 804), onion centre 1349 → 1489. SH03 head top 313 → 279, eyes 600, chin 804 → 829, island far 1328 → 1411. SH04 O4 projected width 438 → 503 px (sheet 433 → 496, +1.2 % / +1.4 %), y 656–912 → 650–948; B02-SH02 l0 is the same camera (whole onion, metric width 459). Caption band (`caption-zone.py --band 1176:1344`) p95: SH01 0.70 (l0–18) → 0.92 (l30–67), SH02 0.92 (l0–16) → 0.64–0.66, SH03 0.63–0.65, SH04 0.62 (l124 0.70); every frame fails, so every G-E caption needs the shade (W5.3, Editor). Essentials vs the band: in SH01/SH02 the face, goggles and hands stay above y 800; the onion halves and slices (centre 1058 → 1489) and the board front pass in and under the band; SH03 band on the apron and chest; SH04 O4 above the band (bottom ≤ 948).

Findings:
- **A kit hold point inside the near plane drops the hold.** `camPoint` returns null under `near`, and the cook's hold block falls back to the pose's own arm; a close face camera whose hands are off frame still needs a small `near` so the arm points the right way out of frame.
- **Kitchen wash flicker under a moving camera (cause suspected, not isolated; not fixed):** visible again in SH01 (fridge wash l30 on) and SH03 (island near edge l45–55). p5.brush (`node_modules/p5.brush/src/fill/fill.js`) seeds each fill, but by reading the source the wash shape also depends on the polygon's screen geometry (candidates: wet-in-wet paper from earlier fills, the paper texture fixed to the canvas, `scatter`'s inside test, `trim`'s spacing sample), so a moving projection re-rolls the bleed; which one dominates was not measured. A fix (painting these washes in a fixed local frame under an affine transform, or dropping their bleed and texture) changes every frame of the approved plates, so it fails the PSNR ≥ 60 dB condition: not applied; waits for a Film/user decision.

Not done: no motion render or seek pass of the four shots (stills only), sound not checked, no commit.

Live hold: SH03 l54 vs l61 26.1 dB (the frames differ: knife and breath move).

SH02 bell draws on l 32–50 at (540, 420) 200 px, then ±4° at 3 Hz. SH03 strokes at l 12, 42, 72 (`cupHold`: rock on the tip as `sliceHold`, the tip 0.095 m in front of O4 at the new cut), 0.012 m trimmed and one slice dropped per stroke; bell ±8° for 18 f per stroke (decaying), idle ±4° between; camera ease-out to l 40 then a 0.02 m drift.

Value and feasibility changes (Boss, AI13; recorded in the s01-b06-b07 change log for Film): B07-SH01/SH02 `hy 600 → 440` (mouth and chin in frame at 600); SH03 dolly end `r 1.70 → 1.68` (lead 476 → 482 px); SH03 gaze `look (−0.15, 1)`; the SH03 bell's lift over the cup's projected top 230 → 200 px so it lands on the sheet's (569, 840) / (722, 640) (the sheet's "top − 150" was measured on the whole-onion sphere).

Measured (`metricsOf`, px): SH01 l0 → l57 O4 top 593 → 591, centre 760 → 754, base 927 → 917, width 425 → 410 (sheet Ø 426); chin −39 (face out of frame), island far 500 → 513. SH02 l0 → l79 O4 top 591 → 641, centre 754 → 826, base 917 → 1010, width 410 → 450 (sheet 426 → 467 at hy 600); bell y ≈ 320–520, clear of O4. SH03 l0 → l84 head top 260 → 346, eyes 477 → 500 (x 540), chin 631 → 611, island far 967 → 828, lead 707 → 482 (sheet 718 → 489); head (top to chin) 371 → 265; O4 centre 1194 → 896, base 1319 → 946 (1144 at l 5, where the caption starts), width 243 → 136; bell (570, 840) 130 px → (725, 646) 90 px, x 653–797 with the marks at the end, face x ≈ 466–614. Caption band (`caption-zone.py --band 1176:1344`) p95: SH01 0.68–0.72, SH02 0.69–0.74, SH03 0.81 (l0), 0.64–0.71 (l5–14), 0.87 (l40–84); every frame fails, so every B07 caption needs the shade (Editor).

Findings:
- **A wash on a new piece flickers like the set washes under a moving camera.** The cup's first contact shadow (`washAt`) changed shape between SH01 l0 and l14 (a dark blob grew at its left); flat translucent layers (`hardFill` on `p.layer(a)`) hold still. New moving-camera pieces use layers for shadow and shade.
- **A longitude that turns away mid-line must split.** Filtering hidden points out of one polyline joined the visible ends across the hidden stretch and drew loops on the belly.
- **Kitchen wash flicker (open, not fixed):** present in B07: SH02 l32 (a brown band along the board's near lip), SH03 l12–l14 (the island's near edge, strongest at l14) and the fridge wash blotches from l40 on. No global fix attempted (G-E note).

Open for Film: the O1 halves at the frame's right edge in SH01/SH02 read as a second, whole onion beside O4 (keep for continuity or drop them from B07-SH01/SH02); the SH03 bell is 90–110 px from l 12 on (small at phone size); at SH03 l0 the bell sits over her knife hand and the handle.

## W4.1-W4.2 voice lock and W1.4 length check (Editor, 2026-11-04)

- Voice: Gemini TTS Achernar (user's liked narrator), atempo 1.08 (EG-EP01 awake style; 1.05 alternative kept). Text `out/edu-voice/text.txt`: script VO with `…` -> `,`, one line per row, L06 omitted (non-verbal), L16 ends on "ว่าตอนที่คุณ" with no closing punctuation.
- Requests: 2 full reads (no audition clips, one per style). Take 1, "soft, warm and close to the mic, hushed and calm like a bedtime explainer, ..., natural everyday pace": 79.16 s raw, too slow (speech plus ~16 s gaps). Take 2 (chosen), style "natural everyday storytelling, soft and warm but clear and conversational, calm quiet wonder, awake, steady brisk pace, short natural pauses, no audible breathing; same narrator throughout, do not speed up": 68.96 s raw, 18.4 s of it silence >= 0.15 s. The slow bedtime style would have failed the length check; keep the brisk style for re-reads.
- Post: [short/voice-regap.py](short/voice-regap.py) (Whisper venv python for numpy): line gap 0.28 s, comma 0.14 s, reveal pauses 0.55 s (before L09 and L12), 3.2 s jolt gap before L07, 0.5 s tail, atempo, loudnorm. Result 59.53 s, -16.2 LUFS, LRA 2.2, TP -1.5. Its `LINE` table holds raw-take silence starts for this take only; a new take needs the table redone.
- Length check (W1.4): 59.53 s vs 40-60 s target (limit 63 s) pass, and it includes the 3.02 s jolt gap placeholder; Preaw trims not needed. Estimate was 49-53 s; the read is longer mainly in B06 (6.7 s vs 4.5 est).
- Measure: [short/narration-measure.json](short/narration-measure.json), onsets from silences, checked against Whisper (faster-whisper in this venv fails on file paths with `av` metadata_errors: pass a decoded numpy array, as `out/edu-voice/tr.py` does). Whisper mis-transcribed some words (Thai-English mix), so alignment uses it only as a +-0.2 s cross-check.
- Open: L06 gasp (narrator Achernar vs separate character voice) not generated; Film sets the jolt gap (fall ~1.5 s + silence ~0.5 s + jolt) and Editor re-gaps; "ฮิปนิก เจิร์ก" read by TTS was not listened to by an agent, user listens at W4.3; Gemini TTS: the style tail "do not speed up" kept.
- Store: `projects/edu/EDU/audio/voice-lock-v1/` holds lock-v1.wav (1.08), alt-x1.05.wav, raw take 2 (+json), raw take 1.

## W5.1 Editor jobs: L06 gasp and phrase-onset check (Editor, 2026-11-04, against EDU-SHEETS-R1)

- Gasp: Gemini TTS (gemini-tts skill), text `<gasp>` only, style `young woman suddenly startled awake, one sharp inhale through the mouth, no words, very short`, three prebuilt voices other than Kore/Achernar: Aoede, Leda, Zephyr (3 requests; raw takes 0.72-0.92 s in `out/edu-gasp/gasp-<Voice>.wav` + cache `$REELS_STORE/cache/tts/`). Not listened to by a human; ranked by Whisper (no words) and by the energy envelope.
- Trim: Aoede 0.21-0.63 s of the raw take (0.42 s), Leda 0.07-0.50 s (0.43 s), Zephyr 0.07-0.55 s (0.48 s); 10 ms fade in, 80 ms fade out, no atempo, mono to stereo 48 kHz 24-bit, peak -1.2 dBFS (library sfx master rule; sheet asked -3 dBFS peak and 3 dB under the narrator, so mix it at timeline volume about -2 dB for peak and about -3 dB for level). Mean level -15.7 / -15.2 / -17.9 dB against the Kore narration's mean -16.1 dB and peak -1.5 dB.
- Whisper on the raw takes: Aoede no words (clean gasp, onset 0.24 s, decays by 0.5 s); Leda hears a Japanese "ha" syllable (breath-like, still no Thai or English word); Zephyr hears "Oh" (word-like, ranked last).
- Filed: `~/ReelsStore/library/sound/sfx/vocal/vocal_gasp-young-woman_01` (Aoede, best), `_02` (Leda), `_03` (Zephyr) with sidecars and INDEX rows. New category `vocal` added to the library README.
- Phrase onsets, method: Whisper `medium` (faster-whisper, Thai, decoded numpy from the Kore take) syllable starts, plus a 10 ms RMS envelope (-40 dB crossing after at least 50 ms of silence). Whisper's first token often snaps to the end of the previous segment, so it reads early where the gap is long; the envelope onset reads late where the phrase starts softly. Both are quoted below; a frame is flagged only when it differs by more than 3 f.

| Onset | Sheet f | Whisper f | Envelope f | Diff > 3 f? | Corrected f |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| L05 p1 | 335 | 333 | 335 | no | |
| L05 p2 | 415 | 410 (snapped to prior end) | 414 | no | |
| L07 p1 | 579 | 579 | 580 | no | |
| L07 p2 | 613 | 617 | 616 (loud body from f622) | yes, +3 to +4 | 617 |
| L11 p1 | 1012 | 1012 | 1012 | no | |
| L11 p2 | 1157 | 1155 | 1157 | no | |
| L13 p1 | 1273 | 1274 | 1274 | no | |
| L13 p2 (dumbbell, "และ") | 1417 | 1415 (snapped) | 1420 | no, borderline +3 | 1419-1420 suggested |
| L15 p1 | 1580 | 1586 | 1590 (faint noise from f1580, speech from f1590) | yes, +6 to +10 | 1586 |
| L15 p2 | 1641 | 1641 | 1641 | no | |
| L13 cup "คาเฟอีน" | 1333 | 1334 | 1333 | no | |
| L13 scribble "ความเครียด" | 1359 | 1358 | 1359 | no | |
| L13 moon+clock "การอดนอน" | 1387 | 1390 | 1390 | no, borderline +3 | 1390 suggested |

- **R1.1 (2026-11-04, Film):** applied the onset check above: L07 p2 f613 -> f617, L13 p2 f1417 -> f1419, L15 p1 f1580 -> f1586, icon 'moon and clock' f1387 -> f1390. Shot boundaries B04-SH01/SH02 609 -> 613, B06-SH01/SH02 1413 -> 1415, B07-SH01/SH02 1576 -> 1582 (each = onset - 4). All 23 shots contiguous, 1860 f unchanged; review-rows.json start/end updated. L05 p2, L11, L13 p1, L07 p1 unchanged (diff <= 3 f).

- L15 p1 and L07 p2: the sheet onset sits on a faint low-level breath (-41 to -46 dB) that precedes the word by 0.2 to 0.3 s. Built (kit intake KIT-P01 `new:` names): `new: sit on bed edge` and `new: lie-back motion` (lying torso rotates about hip PIV (720,916), rot 0..1, arms/hand/hug), `new: jolt motion` (lift 56 px, drop, -6 overshoot, blanket jump, wide eyes, 3 f flash), lying-head `wide`/open-eye almond/`smile`, wallSoft mist 0.25..0.62, room-to-cloud bleed (dream layer over bedroom, ledge late), shared dim curve (`dimCurve`, DimLayer multiply + saturation overlay), bowed head `roll`/`dip` (nod L8-18), back-view lean + slip off frame, `new: cloud-air ground`, `new: upward paint-streak FX`, `new: falling pose in pyjama`, `new: idea bubble`, `new: brain-cloud`, `new: sun/moon switch`, `new: relaxed mini-figure`, icon pops with fade-to-60 %, doctor nod. Not done / honest limits: (1) the sit-up torso is a rotated slab with a short neck and a floating-looking head (needs a kit seated/long-sit body); (2) kit gaps 1-3 (kit `bowed` head, back heel, female torso cues) NOT moved into `src/painted/kit`, still episode-local; (3) the faller keeps the kit ground shadow under the feet (no shadow-off); (4) B03-SH01 mid-dissolve shows faint pencil lines of the ledge; (5) `S01-B01-SH02` eyes look along the face (profile), not at the lens; (6) caption-zone fails every shot (mean 0.33-0.90), shade at W5.3; (7) no seek/sequence parity run and no whole-clip render (stills only); (8) tag, name card, captions, sound are W5.3.

B07-SH01: pillow modelled (shade, light, seam, creases) and the near arm lies over it. She sits mid-bed with legs under the blanket (not on the mattress edge): the kit has no long-sit body, so a kit-intake body is still open.

- Not fixed: faller hair does not stream upward (kit hair is fixed); nits (B02-SH01 repeat framing, B05 head tilt, drip signal, doctor coat/hands) untouched; caption-zone still fails (W5.3 shade); no motion/seek check of the new sit-up arc (stills only).

### W5.2 repaint round 3 (2026-11-04, Boss, user: "extra head layer comes up with her when she wakes")
- **Duplicated layer:** the hair fan that lies spread on the pillow (`paintLyingHead` step 1, drawn under the head) kept its full lying extent while she rose, so it read as a second dark head-shaped blob behind and below the real head, rising with her (f584-612, f1752-1830). Not a second head: one head, one neck, one torso were drawn at every frame.
- **Fix:** the fan draws in toward the head with `rot` (u x 0.55, v x 0.7 upright) so it becomes a bob behind the head; fan colour already moves to the dark hair tone. ## W5.3 Editor Draft assembly (2026-11-04, Editor)

Inputs: sheets EDU-SHEETS-R1.1, script EDU-SCRIPT-R1.1, voice Kore v1, composition `EDU-EP01-SHORT`. Reproduce: `store.py fetch`; `out/voice-venv/bin/python -I scripts/edu-series/short/sound-mix.py out/edu01/w53` (writes the stem, `EDU-EP01-SHORT-mix-check.wav`, `cues.json`); `node scripts/storyboard-page/clips.mjs EDU-EP01-SHORT scripts/edu-series/short/review-rows.json --shots=<all ids> --draft` and `--full`; `python3 -I scripts/storyboard-page/build.py scripts/edu-series/short/review-rows.json`. Code: `src/painted/edu01/captions.ts` (cues, centre y 1164, shade 0.30), `src/painted/shots/edu01.tsx` (CaptionShade + name card in the Overlay), `src/videos/edu-ep01/index.ts` (narration, stem, label cues, captionStyle `centerY` 1164), `tests/edu01.test.ts` (+1 test, 464/464).

- **Why one sound stem:** `video.audio[]` has volume and fades only. The sheet needs frame-exact gain arcs, a digital-zero gap on every layer and a pad/clock that agree across f1860 -> f0. The stem is 62.000 s, built from library files with gain curves; the timeline plays it as one track beside the `narration`. Cost: no `duckUnderNarration` (the beds sit 15 LU under the voice instead) and a change needs a rebuild + new store file (stem v1 and v2 are superseded, v3 is current).
- **Level reference (finding):** the mono Kore wav plays at -16.1 LUFS in the render (the renderer pans it equal-power); my first stem assumed +3 dB (dual mono) and was 3 dB hot. Calibrated on the render: narration -16.1, mix -16.2 LUFS (render), stem -31.3 LUFS.
- **Pad:** `edu1_pad_v1` (72 s generation, seed 4101) cut 3-72 s, dynaudnorm, 6 s equal-power loop crossfade to exactly 62.000 s, static gain (ffmpeg loudnorm in dynamic mode broke the seam, first pass: step 0.05 vs 0.0004 typical, caught by a sample check). Stem seam: first/last 50 ms RMS -43.3 / -42.3 dB. Clock: one tick of `tension_clock-pulse_01` on a 62/67 s grid so it is periodic across the seam (the library loop is 9.8 s, not 62 s).
- **Caption wording:** script Sub text, not the sheet's shot lines, where they differ (L02 "?", L10 and L11 shortened, L13 p2 without "และการ", L15 without "แต่"/"นะ", L07 p1 without "…"). Open for Film/Preaw: (1) script Sub for L07 is the Latin "hypnic jerk" subtitle; the sheet puts Latin on the name card and keeps the Thai VO read in the caption (used), so the card's Thai gloss "อาการกระตุกตอนเคลิ้มหลับ" and the caption's 2nd line say the same words at the same time; (2) the L16 caption stays to f1860 ("…" into the seam) while the sheet's shade ends L95; shade follows the caption.
- **Motion checks (frames from stills, sheets in `out/edu01/w53/`: `qa-jolt.png`, `qa-situp.png`, `qa-lieback.png`, `qa-faller.png`, `qa-n7.png`, `seam-4.png`):** jolt B03-SH05: hair-top y 741 rest, 3-frame flash f550-552 washes the hair, 714 at f552, 685 at f555 (lift 56 px, as designed), 747 at f562 (6 px overshoot), 741 from f569. Sit-up f579-613: one continuous rise over about 20 f, no pop at 150 px tiles, but it reads as a rigid slab pivoting about the hip with a long thin neck. Lie-back f1750-1830: continuous, reverse of the sit-up, same stiffness. N7 f596-602 at 270 px: hand moves a few px per frame, no visible pop. Faller f490-532: tumbles +-12 deg with arm swing and camera drop; the streaks are soft coloured pills drifting up (read as paint drops, weak speed lines); hair does not stream (known). Face tint reads grey-green on the seated figure.
- **Finding for Boss (blocker for the seam):** `C_seamless` last-frame behaviour. Stills f1836-1858 stay 23-29 dB PSNR from f0 (no convergence to f0 over the 15 f window) and f1859 is f0 (65.9 dB): a one-frame hard pop between f1858 and f1859 (1858 vs 1859 = 29 dB). Two causes seen: B08-SH01's end state differs from B01-SH01 f0 (camera framing, bed/pillow brightness, clock glow: `seam-4.png` f1857, f1858, f1859, f0), and the plate blend in `paintedStateAt` does not visibly fade f0 in over `loopBlendFrames` 15 for this composition. The sheet requires the last frame of B08 to equal f0; this needs Boss (plate end state) and a runtime check of the blend with painted plates. The final cut and the loop cut at f1859 -> f0 are exact; the pop is f1858 -> f1859.
- **Seek parity:** 13 frames rendered forward and again shuffled/backward with `render.mjs --stills`: 5 byte-identical, 8 differ by isolated pixels (PSNR 105-116 dB). Studio scrub was not run (no interactive Studio in this session).
- **Not done / not claimed:** no one has listened to any sound, the mix or the gasp placement (measured only); no Studio play/pause/scrub; B03-SH04 digital zero holds in the stem (exact) but the AAC render shows -87.6 dB RMS, -61 dB peak in f535-549 (codec ringing); name card not judged at 360 px beyond the Draft clip (see contact sheet if added); `tsc` shows 3 pre-existing errors in `tests/` (loudness.mjs typing, `render-options.test.ts` `covers`), none in `src/`.

### W5.3 loop-seam fix (2026-11-05, Boss)
- Cause (measured with stills f1830-1859): B08's end state already equals B01 f0 (picture area 54 dB). The `C_seamless` blend made it worse: (1) frame 0's ink is laid at partial alpha and faded ink mixes toward paper, so the picture bleached to 23 dB mid-blend; (2) `edu01` Overlay ignored its `opacity` prop, so grade, clock glow and grain of both plates stacked at full strength (double glow, brighter pillow).
- Fix: `PaintedShot.loopExact` (src/painted/shot.ts; set on B01-SH01) makes `PaintedStage` skip frame 0's ink until the last frame (`PaintedPlate.incoming` from `paintedStateAt`); the Overlay honours `opacity` for the shade, and the shared screen layers hand over whole on the last frame (B08 off, B01 on; same grade/glow/dim, test added). `loopBlendFrames` stays 15. Other videos are unchanged (field is opt-in).
- Result (full frame): step f1858 to f1859 51.9 dB (typical step 45-50), f1858 vs f0 52.2 dB, f1859 vs f0 65.9 dB, no plate step in f1845-1859. Evidence `out/edu01/seam/seam-sheet.png`.
- Open (Editor): captions of the last scene are hidden by the renderer from f1845 (`entering` while the blend runs), so the trailing "..." cue vanishes in one frame (step 24 dB at f1844 to f1845) instead of staying into the seam. Fix is in SceneRenderer/caption logic, not touched.

## W5.7 caption fade

- Problem: SceneRenderer hid the last scene's captions when the C_seamless loop blend opened (f1844), so the final cue vanished in one frame.
- Fix: new opt-in `captionStyle.fadeOutWithLoop` (types.ts, validated in media.ts). When set, the final scene keeps its captions through the blend and `Subtitles` takes an `opacity` of `max(0, 1 - 1.25 * blendOpacity)`: full at f1844, gone by f1855, so the faint-ghost PSNR cost at the seam is nil. Off by default; other videos unchanged. EDU-EP01-SHORT sets it in src/videos/edu-ep01/index.ts.
- Evidence: seam PSNR f1858 vs f0 52.24 dB (52.28 without the change; f1859 64.86 vs 65.73), stills out/EDU-EP01-SHORT/w57-fade-sheet.png, draft clip out/EDU-EP01-SHORT/clips/S01-B08-SH01_draft.mp4. npm test 465/465 pass.

Boss, 5 Nov 2026, from Film's viewer check Draft r1 ([viewer-check-draft-r1.md](ep02/viewer-check-draft-r1.md)). Plates only, `src/painted/edu02`; not committed; Draft clips not re-rendered. ## EP02 W6 Final fix (Boss; user N1 and N2 on the Final cut, 2026-11-05)

- **N1 cause:** the Draft path wraps the brush so `fill` becomes a flat `wash` (`src/painted/BrushCanvas.tsx`, `drafted`). The Final kept textured `fill`. Its bleed and texture come from random draws along the polygon's projected vertices (`node_modules/p5.brush/src/fill/fill.js`, `grow` and `layer`), so under the moving yaw camera the same seed gives a new bleed each frame. The seeds are stable; no time term or random grain is involved. Held shots matched the Draft; moving shots flickered at 1.3–2.4× the Draft's sign alternation.
- **N1 fix:** `src/painted/edu02/flat.ts` (`flatFillBrush`, `flatPainter`) is an episode-local copy of `drafted`. `src/painted/shots/edu02.tsx` paints with it, and `ShotPlate` and `DissolvePlate` in `shots.tsx` use it for QA stills. No shared file changed, so other episodes' pixels and cache keys should not change (reasoned, not measured).
- **N2 cause:** the cook's left hand was painted twice at the wick. The rig paints its open hand (`cast.ts` `openHandParts`), and `handItem` (`kitchen.ts`) repainted a second open palm at the same wrist, turned +0.5 rad, plus the hand's thumb lobe. At l30 the hand also jumped 0.12 m down for one frame, and the rig hand was hidden behind the candle there, so the repainted palm and its thumb hung below as a second palm.
- **N2 fix:** `CookState.lAirHand` (opt-in, set only by S01-B05-SH03) makes `handItem` paint the rig's own hand geometry and drops the thumb lobe on both hands. The return starts from the lifted point, and the hand drops late (height follows back²) so it clears the flame.
- **Measurement:** the system `python3` has no numpy; `out/edu02/w6/diag/allshots.py` ran in a scratch venv with numpy 2.5.4 (`pip install numpy`). Next time either install numpy into a project venv or record the interpreter in `scripts/README.md`.
- **Housekeeping:** `render.mjs --stills` overwrote `out/EDU-EP02-SHORT/stills/{330,540,1080}.png`; Boss moved them to `out/edu02/w6/diag/timeline/`, so those stills are missing from the stills cache until re-rendered.
- **Proposed, not applied (DP01):** a dev-findings entry "a textured p5.brush fill shimmers under a moving camera even with a fixed seed; use the flat wash for moving camera shots". For the user to approve.
- **Film to accept (AI13):** the B05-SH03 hand path change (`scenes/s01-b05.md`, W6 Final fix line).

## EP03 W6 Final fix (Boss; user: "I want the Draft picture, drop the brush look of Final", 6 Nov 2026)

- **Difference Draft vs Final (painted shots):** only the brush wrapper `drafted` in `src/painted/BrushCanvas.tsx` (input prop `paintDraft`): textured `fill` (bleed + texture) becomes a flat `wash`, `fillBleed`/`fillTexture` are dropped, `hatch` is off. Grade, grain, vignette, dog view and screen layers are the same in both. Cause of the flicker is the textured fill (EP02 W6 diagnosis, same code): bleed/texture draw from the seeded random stream walked along the projected vertices, so under the moving camera the same seed gives new marks each frame. The see-through dog of B01-SH02 is the same cause (soft bleed marks over a low-alpha body); with flat washes it is opaque.
- **Fix (EP03 only):** `src/painted/shots/edu03.tsx` paints with `flatPainter(p, EDU3_PAPER)` (reuses `src/painted/edu02/flat.ts`), and `ShotPlate` in `src/painted/edu03/shots.tsx` uses `flatFillBrush` so stills equal the timeline. Always on for EDU3 plates, so Final = Draft picture (and the Draft is unchanged: wrapping an already-flat brush is a no-op). No shared file changed; EP01/EP02/other episodes untouched. Caption shade, dog view, tag, sounds untouched. EDU3 scene-cache keys change (source hash), so a Final render repaints every scene.
- **Evidence** (`out/edu03/w6b/`): `sheet_B01SH02_final_new_draft.png` and `sheet_B04SH02_final_new_draft.png` (rows: old Final, new Final, Draft; f100-103 and f1000-1003). B01-SH02 dog opaque in the new Final. PSNR new vs Draft 42.3-43.2 dB (B01-SH02 f100-103) and 42.3-42.5 dB (B04-SH02 f1000-1001), old Final vs Draft 22.9-23.3 dB. Consecutive-frame PSNR f100->101: Draft 30.68, new 30.67, old 31.85 (B04-SH02 f1000->1001: 25.23 / 25.23 / 24.90); the old Final's boil is in the marks, not the whole-frame average, so the visual sheets are the main evidence. The old Final B04-SH02 also had a dark blotch at the pit that is gone. Clips: `out/EDU-EP03-SHORT/clips/S01-B01-SH02_final.full.mp4`, `S01-B04-SH02_final.full.mp4`.
- **EP02 check:** `render.mjs EDU-EP02-SHORT --stills --frames=330,900` with and without the patch: PSNR 69.3 / 74.2 dB between states, the same as two runs of one state (69.1 / 74.5 dB): renderer noise, md5 is not stable on this machine.
- **Editor command:** `node scripts/storyboard-page/clips.mjs EDU-EP03-SHORT scripts/edu-series/ep03/review-rows.json --full --timeout=240000`. `npm test` 490/490 (`out/logs/ep03-flat-npm-test.log`). The two test clips took 715 s for 2 shots at Final (the dog-view backdrop and rendering whole 3-scene segments); the Draft path would be fastest, but the flat fills now make both the same picture.

## W10 Closing fade of the bed (Editor, 2026-11-06, user listened to W9: "the tag is OK, but the bird and ambience sound should also fade to zero as the clip is about to end")

- `ep03/sound-mix.py` now fades every layer except narration and the closing tag (guitar bed, birds, pad, sfx) from c1482 (last word's end) to exact zero at the last sample: gain dB = -90 * t^1.5, t 0..1 over 3.0 s (zero slope at the start, no cliff; about -28 dB at 1.5 s). A first try with cos^2 left the tail at -52 dBFS, so it was replaced. Tag keeps its own envelope (2.2 s, 0.5 s fade, done by c1548).

- Measured (stem, 0.25 s RMS): 50.5 s -30.8, 50.75 -39.5, 51.0 -55, 51.25 -69.6, 51.5 -85.6, 51.75 -97.7, 52.0 -106, 52.25 -127 dBFS; last 0.3 s peak 1.4e-5 (-97 dBFS), last sample 0. Narration + stem check mix: -13.5 LUFS, peak -0.2 dBFS before render normalisation, identical to W9 (the fade does not touch the loud part). Images: `out/w10/tail-before.png`, `out/w10/tail-after.png` (spectrogram + waveform, last 6 s).
- Hard restart at c0 now follows silence (user's loop decision; picture seam unchanged). Not listened to. Re-render after the commit: `render.mjs` (all scenes reused, no repaint); the delivered `f6a118f6e668` render is superseded for sound only.

| LF03 | S01-B04-SH02 (night) | same cast, `surprised` (VEN) and `surprised` + eyes right-to-left look (CUS); night grade `n = 1` | starry sky, Moon lit with no halo, cool crescent rim on both heads, no warm or electric light, pestle frozen at the top of the stroke | camera az 15 r 3.0 eye 1.25 pitch 0 f 1150 hy 900; VEN head unit 132 px, CUS 233 px; mean luma 0.229, 0.0 % of pixels under 0.05 (UT03 criterion 0.12 / 35 % met); caption band mean 0.28 / p95 0.45 |

Differences from the brief (W3 tuning, recorded; Film owns the brief): (1) the shophouse row is not painted: at pose A (camera on the +x side looking toward -x) it falls outside the frame; towers sit at z -250 (z -80 made them fill the sky); (2) the mortar is at x 0.66 and VEN at x 0.20 (the pestle in front of her face hid it); her screen-right arm grips the pestle, the other rests on the rim; (3) stool 2 is left of the cart, not at the road side (it blocked the foreground); (4) SET-SOI pose A camera is nearer and longer than the brief (`r 3.7`, `f 1480` against `r 2.80 / 3.15`, `f 1050`): the brief's lead sizes could not both hold with CUS 1.3 m nearer the lens than VEN; this keeps VEN at 149 px (spike 1 asks 150) and CUS at 253 px; (5) LF04 elevation 46 deg (brief 32): the vertical frame filled better; (6) LF02 draws the ring at R 0.10 su with the card at 08:19: the true radius at the first card frame is about 0, so the ring is exaggerated for legibility (alternative: the card at 07:29 for the same ring); (7) LF02 street panel `hy 960` (brief 1250) so the heads stay above y 1160.

Defects found and fixed while building: the `man-crop` automatic blink lands on t 0 (eyes shut at frame 0): CUS is drawn at `t + 1.3` in the look frames, so W5 must pick the shot-local blink phase (the loop-seam frame needs both people's blink phases equal at frame 0 and the last frame). `washAt` polygons render as faceted flat shapes (flat fills): the nebula and the Sun halo are screen-layer gradients, as the skill says. `p5.brush` small circles render as squares: stars are 8-point `ell` polygons.

Not done (deferred to W5, listed in the brief's N1-N10): day-night grade as a continuous function (only the two states exist), SET-GW, Jupiter, the light-dash tail following the ring, ring/clock link, `fan-self`, `point-up`, the motorbike and the shophouse row, dust motes, the loop-seam check, the pestle cycle (LF01 and LF03 are single stroke phases 0.62 and 1.0), sound. Caption zone: the brief's edu stroke handles bright backgrounds; the default white-outline thresholds fail on LF01 and LF02 (as EP01-EP06 did); the decision stays with W5.

Determinism: LF01 and LF03 rendered twice, max channel difference 12 / 255 on 0.04 % of channels (2.7k and 3.3k of 6.2M), not the 1/255 of earlier looks; not investigated (stroke edges). ESLint clean on `src/painted/edu07`; `tsc` no errors in edu07; `scripts/context-map.py` passes with the new EP07 row.

## ep07 W3 look frames v2 (2026-11-08, Boss for Film; user feedback on v1)

Stills `out/edu07/look-frames/v2/` (`LF01`-`LF04`, `LF01-shadow-poc.png`, sheets `sheet-v2-pestle-shadow.png` = LF01 v1 / v2 / shadow POC / LF03 v2, `sheet-v2-all.png`). Render: `out/logs/lf-v2.txt` through `node scripts/stills.mjs src/painted/edu07/look-index.ts` (one Chrome, 5 stills 7.6 s). Source: `soi.ts`, `space.ts`, `scenes.ts`, `look.tsx`, `look-index.ts` (new composition `EDU7-LF01Poc`).

- Pestle (user 1, 2): now the Thai wooden krok, 0.5 m, a bulged rounded head at the hitting end (bottom, 4.8 cm radius) tapering to a thin handle, lit stripe on the right. Grip: her left (pounding) hand is a fist closed around the upper handle (fingers wrapped across the front, thumb over, butt end above the fist), her right hand rests flat on the mortar rim with the fingers hooked over the lip (steadying). Shared by LF01, LF03 and W5 (`PESTLE_LEN`, `gripAt`).
- Brush shadow POC (user 3): `paintSoi(..., {shadow: 'brush'})` replaces the flat ground shadows (umbrella, cart + cabinet, both stools, CUS, VEN, crate) with a translucent wash + charcoal hatching + wobbly colour-pencil edge, light from the right (`LX -0.6, LZ -0.24`). Baseline (`shadow` absent) is unchanged.
- LF02 ring: unchanged from v1 (user, 8 Nov 2026: keep the exaggerated ring and the 08:19 card; the thinner ring was dropped). LF04: Earth x1.4 (`earthK`), 154 px diameter.
- Lead height unchanged from v1 (same cameras): LF01 CUS head unit 253 px, VEN 149 px; LF03 233 / 132 px.

### ep07 LF01 shadow POC round 2: watercolour (2026-11-08, user: "pencil hatching is not what I want")

`out/edu07/look-frames/v2/LF01-shadow-watercolour.png`, sheet `sheet-v2-shadows-3way.png` (flat v2 / pencil POC / watercolour POC, 360 px). p5.brush does have a watercolour fill (`fill` + `fillBleed` + `fillTexture`), but `BrushCanvas` turns every textured fill into a flat wash (flat-fill rule, 6 Nov), so the POC reads the unwrapped brush from a new optional `PaintContext.textured` (BrushCanvas.tsx, one line; scenes that ignore it are unaffected). `paintSoi(..., {shadow: 'watercolour', wc})` paints each shadow in three layers: soft wide wash (bleed 0.55, texture 0.6), a smaller darker offset wet-in-wet pool (bleed 0.7), a thin pooled rim; hulls smoothed by two Chaikin passes. Composition `EDU7-LF01Wc`. Cost: 3.0-3.2 s per still vs 2.8-3.9 s flat (Chrome startup dominates, no measurable per-frame difference at 6 polygons x 3 layers). Determinism: two renders differ in 0.07 % of PNG filter bytes (v1 baseline 0.04 %), so seeded and still. Flicker under a moving camera is NOT tested: the 6 Nov finding says textured fills flicker when the camera moves, so a W5 shot with a camera move needs a moving-camera check before adopting. Texture grain is canvas-fixed, so it would swim on a dolly.

Caption legibility (the sheet's open check) on bright frames G28, G230, G308, G370, G480, G940, G1730: viewed at 720 px, white glyphs with the dark stroke are legible on the pale street; ink spans y 1264-1329 on one line cues and 1190-1316 on the two-line L03, inside the 1176-1344 band. `caption-zone.py` mean 0.52-0.64 on those frames (the number includes the caption's own white glyphs, p95 0.79-1.0), so the 0.25 default fails as already known for the day street; no darkening, band move or per-cue change was made.

## EP07 W5.3 fix round 1 (2026-11-08, Boss)

Input: `ep07/fixlist.md` (F1-F6). Stills only (`scripts/stills.mjs`, `scripts/qa-sheet.sh`), no video render, not committed. All in `src/painted/edu07/soi.ts` plus one flag in `shots/S01-B04-SH05.tsx`; IDs, ranges and timing unchanged.

- **F1 (S01-B02-SH03), my reading of the cut-off note:** "the vendor's hand floats away from the pestle". Cause: her pounding arm clamps at its reach (`reachTo` in `kit/cast.ts`) when the pestle is lifted, so the arm's hand stopped about 45 px (at 400 px tile) short of the fist drawn on the handle. Fix: the grip slides down the handle as it rises (`gripS`, hand never above y 1.45 m); arm hand and fist now coincide (crop checked at G355). Same fix applies to every shot with the raised pestle.
- **F5/F6 (B05-SH02, B05-SH03, plus B02-SH02, B01-SH01, B03-SH01):** cause: the front-half ring of the mortar wrapped the array end, so the wall polygon (front rim + reversed front base) twisted and left the pestle head painted over the mortar. `frontHalf` now rotates the ring to start at the first front point. Checked G1619-1633, G1685-1703: pestle hidden inside the mortar.
- **F2 (B04-SH04):** `paintMoon` night polish: soft glow, thin limb, uneven terminator, maria and craters on the lit half, faint maria on the earthshine half. Gated to `night > 0.5` so the small day Moon keeps its approved plain disc (checked G0, G100, G1010). Fades with `moon` as before (G1320 checked).
- **F3 (B04-SH04):** the rim was a long band fixed to the right of each head (outside the hair on CUS, the wrong side for a Moon up-left). `moonRim` now draws a thin tapered crescent hugging the hair outline toward the Moon's screen position. It applies to every night shot that uses `rim` (B04-SH01..03 not re-viewed individually beyond the G940/G1010 sheet).
- **F4 (B04-SH05):** new `ven.finger` option draws an index finger (tapered, outlined, along shoulder-to-hand) on the raised hand, shown from L36 as she points; checked G1380, G1429.
- **Measured:** seam G1797 vs G0 PSNR 36.80 dB (W5.2: 36.82); Moon and rim changes do not touch G0/G1797 (day).
- **Evidence:** `out/ep07-w53/sheet-A-fixed-shots.png` (15 frames, 5 shots), `sheet-B-pestle-neighbours.png`, `sheet-C2-night-moon.png`, `sheet-D-day-moon-check.png`; before frames `out/ep07-w53/before/`.
- **Limits:** `tsc`/lint not run; stills only; pestle grip slide not checked in motion; the finger is a flat shape (no knuckle).
